# Remix icons

[All icon packs](../README.md)

Copy a qualified name into `icon = "remix:name"`. Generated from `icons/remix.luau`.

Remix icons are © Remix Design, Apache-2.0 (upstream: https://github.com/Remix-Design/RemixIcon).
